LLM serving pipeline 有启发，尤其是 diffusion LM 作为辅助模型，而不是直接替代主模型的路径。","technical_approach":"LibraSpec 把每轮 speculative length 的选择写成期望加速比优化。核心不是先估计最终能接受多少 token，而是判断“再多验证一段候选 token 是否能带来足够的边际收益”。论文定义了 acceptance gain 与 verification cost 的比值，推导出 beneficial adjustment 的必要充分条件，并证明在其速度模型下，expected speedup 关于 speculative length 呈单峰结构。基于这个性质，算法可以从当前长度出发迭代做有益调整，有限步收敛到全局最优长度区间。实现上不训练额外 predictor，而是直接使用 diffusion drafter 输出的 confidence score 来近似验证前不可得的接受概率，因此可以插到现有 diffusion speculative decoding 方法中。","experiments":"实验覆盖 6 个 LLM、3 种 diffusion-based speculative decoding 方法，并在数学推理、代码生成、聊天任务上测试，同时包含 greedy decoding 和 sampling setting。主要结果是 LibraSpec 相比原有 diffusion speculative decoding 进一步提升端到端加速比约 0.5 到 1.5 倍；相对普通自回归解码最高达到 8.49 倍加速。论文还用 oracle exhaustive search 的最优长度作参照，显示 LibraSpec 能较好跟踪每个 decoding round 的最优投机长度变化。关键消融点在于：仅预测接受长度不足以最大化速度，因为 diffusion drafter 的候选生成成本已不是主要瓶颈；直接使用边际收益准则能更好处理“高接受概率但验证收益不够”的候选后缀。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 20 到 30 分钟。重点看 Introduction 中对 AR drafter 和 diffusion drafter 成本结构差异的分析，再看 Methodology 里 expected speedup、beneficial adjustment、单峰性证明和迭代长度选择算法。实验部分主要看 oracle length 对比图、不同任务和不同 decoding setting 下的端到端 speedup。若关注 serving，可以深挖它的成本模型假设：target verification latency 是否线性随长度增长、batching 和 KV-cache 行为是否会改变边际准则。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies dynamic speculative-length selection for diffusion-based speculative decoding. Prior dynamic methods usually estimate how many drafted tokens will be accepted, which matches autoregressive drafters because drafting itself is sequential. With diffusion drafters, candidate blocks are generated in parallel at much lower cost, so the main decision becomes how many already-generated candidates are worth verifying with the target model.","importance":"The contribution is mainly on inference rather than pretraining, but it is relevant for base-model researchers tracking non-AR language models. It clarifies a practical role for diffusion LMs: they may serve as efficient block drafters inside an AR target model pipeline. The useful point is the changed cost structure. Once drafting becomes cheap, verification cost and wasted suffix computation after early rejection dominate the length-selection problem.","technical_approach":"LibraSpec formulates length selection as expected-speedup optimization instead of accepted-length prediction. The method evaluates whether extending the speculative prefix improves the ratio between acceptance gain and verification cost. The paper derives necessary and sufficient conditions for a beneficial length adjustment and proves that, under its speed model, expected speedup is unimodal with respect to the speculative length. This allows iterative local adjustments to monotonically improve speedup and converge in finite steps to a globally optimal length interval. In implementation, LibraSpec is training-free. It uses confidence scores from the diffusion drafter as proxies for acceptance-related quantities that are unavailable before verification, and can be plugged into existing diffusion speculative decoding systems.","experiments":"The evaluation uses six LLMs and three diffusion-based speculative decoding methods, covering math reasoning, code generation, and chat benchmarks under both greedy and sampling settings. LibraSpec improves end-to-end speedup over the underlying diffusion speculative decoding methods by about 0.5x to 1.5x, and reaches up to 8.49x speedup over vanilla autoregressive decoding. The paper also compares against an oracle length obtained by exhaustive search and shows that LibraSpec closely tracks the round-wise optimal length. The key experimental message is that accepted-length estimation alone is not enough: a likely accepted suffix can still be a poor choice if its marginal verification cost is too high.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Focus on the introduction’s distinction between AR and diffusion drafter cost structures, then read the methodology around expected speedup, beneficial adjustment, unimodality, and the iterative length-selection rule. In the experiments, look for the oracle-length comparison and end-to-end speedup tables across tasks and decoding modes. For serving work, the most important part to inspect is the latency model: assumptions about verification cost, sequence length scaling, batching, and KV-cache behavior determine how portable the marginal criterion is.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看边际收益推导和成本模型；若验证开销建模干净，可直接迁移到内部推测解码 ablation。","en":"Read the marginal-gain derivation and cost model; if verification cost is measured cleanly, it is directly reusable for internal speculative-decoding ablations."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Zexun Lin","Yuan Feng","Junlin Lv","Kevin S. Zhou","Xike Xie"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["speculative-decoding","diffusion-drafter","inference-acceleration","dynamic-length","serving"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08721","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.08721","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.08910","title":"Tied Trit-Planes: Constraining PTQTP to a Uniform Nine-Level Quantizer, with a Persistent Folded Format for Disk-Streamed Mixture-of-Experts Serving","abstract":"PTQTP decomposes LLM weight matrices into two ternary (trit) planes with two free per-group scales. Tying the scales to a fixed ratio of three collapses the decomposition into a single uniform nine-level quantizer, a known balanced-ternary identity. To our knowledge, at the time of writing, this work is the first to impose that identity as a constraint inside PTQTP's solver. The two trit planes then fold losslessly into one 4-bit code plane that we make the persistent serving representation: dis","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-09T20:53:47Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_08910.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体但很现实的 serving 问题：超大 MoE 模型在消费级硬件上部署时，专家参数远大于内存，必须从 SSD 流式读取；这时吞吐主要受“每个 token 需要搬多少字节”控制，而质量则受量化误差控制。作者关心的核心不是再发明一种抽象量化理论，而是：能否把 PTQTP 这种双 ternary-plane 的后训练量化，约束成一个统一的 9-level 量化器，从而把专家权重永久存成单一 4-bit 代码格式，做到磁盘、缓存、kernel 输入三处字节完全一致，避免任何转码，同时把质量损失控制在可接受范围内。","importance":"对基座预训练本身的直接价值有限，它更偏推理系统与量化部署；但对大规模 MoE 的研究工程仍有现实启发。第一，它说明在 MoE serving 场景里，量化格式设计不能只看 perplexity，还要看 persistent format、I/O 路径和 kernel 兼容性，尤其是专家从 NVMe 流式加载时，格式是否需要中间转码会直接影响端到端延迟。第二，它给出一个明确取舍：把 PTQTP 的自由双尺度约束成 ratio-3 tied scales，会损失一部分重构自由度，但换来统一 9-level code 和更干净的系统实现。这个取舍对未来做 MoE-friendly quantization 或训练时感知部署格式的人有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法核心是把 PTQTP 的两层 ternary 分解\nW≈α1T1+α2T2，约束为 α=(3s,s)。这样组合码 c=3t1+t2，t1,t2∈{-1,0,1}，自然落在一个均匀间隔的 9-level 网格上。原本 PTQTP 每个 group 有两个自由 scale，可以拟合更灵活的非均匀九级量化；作者这里主动放弃这部分自由度，换来一个单尺度、均匀九级的表示。由于 9 个有效值可以塞进 4 bit code，两个 ternary plane 就能无损折叠成一个 4-bit composite code plane。\n\n真正有意思的是系统落地。作者不是只在论文里说“可以 pack 成 int4”，而是把这种 folded 4-bit code 直接作为 persistent serving representation：磁盘上的专家文件、内存中的 expert cache、以及 CPU-SIMD kernel 的输入，三者使用同一份字节布局，不做 transcode。文中实现了名为 tq2_0_fx4 的 block format：每个 block 覆盖四列乘 256 个元素，总大小 520 bytes，对应 4.0625 bits/weight。这样做的系统收益是，expert projection miss 时只需要一次连续读取，NVMe 分层缓存也能按 block 粒度直接条带化热点前缀，而不是先读压缩格式再转成另一种 kernel-friendly 格式。\n\n质量评估上，作者没有只报 perplexity，而是强调与官方 serving API 的 step-level reference fidelity。具体做法是用 greedy decoding，在捕获的 fixtures 上比较 decoded-text prefix agreement，而不是 token id 完全一致；同时设置一个“expert-lossless serving arm”作为经验控制组，即把官方发布的 MXFP4 专家字节重打包但不再量化，用来锚定误差主要来自专家量化而不是其他系统因素。作者还提到 process isolation protocol，因为他们测到 fixture 级别存在不稳定性。这些细节说明工作重点是部署可用性，而不是离线量化指标。","experiments":"从全文可见，实验围绕一个 284B-A13B 的生产级 MoE 模型展开，专家部分参数量极大，作者给出的 serving 文件中约 145 GiB 是 q4_k 级别的专家 payload，总 serving 文件约 153.3 GiB，trunk 和 embedding 约 8 GiB。量化对象是官方发布 checkpoint 的专家权重；作者特别说明该 post-trained artifact 公开可得的是 MXFP4 专家，而不是更高精度版本，因此评测是围绕真实可部署工件进行的。\n\n实验比较的重点不是单一 benchmark 分数，而是三条轴：重构/困惑度、与官方 API 的输出一致性、以及实际 serving 速度。作者明确指出一个核心经验结论：这些轴并不总是一致。也就是说，perplexity 更好不一定意味着 step-level reference fidelity 更高，后者更高也不必然带来更好的下游问答分数。这一点对量化研究很重要，因为很多工作默认离线 perplexity 足够代表部署质量。\n\n系统侧，作者声称 ratio-3 tied nine-level folded code 让 persistent bytes、cache bytes、kernel input bytes 完全一致，这正是速度结果成立的前提。文中还把它与 q4_k 等现有格式放在同一部署环境里比较，并使用 CPU-first、可选 Metal/CUDA GEMM offload 的开源栈 fucina 实现。虽然给出的节选里没有完整数值表，但从正文叙述可知，作者把“无转码 SSD 流式专家 serving”作为主要卖点，并用官方 API 对齐、MMLU 描述性比较和 perplexity 并列报告，强调质量判断不能只看单一指标。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合带着系统问题去读，而不是当成通用量化论文精读。先看 Introduction，抓住两个判断：一是作者的贡献很窄，主要是把 PTQTP 约束成 tied ratio-3 nine-level quantizer；二是他们真正关心的是 persistent format 与 serving path 的统一，而不是单纯压低 bit 数。\n\n然后重点看方法部分里关于 balanced ternary identity、folded 4-bit code 和 tq2_0_fx4 block format 的定义。这里要想清楚：为什么自由双尺度 PTQTP 更灵活，但作者仍愿意加约束；以及为什么“磁盘字节=缓存字节=kernel 输入字节”在 SSD-streamed MoE 里比多一点离线重构精度更值钱。\n\n实验部分建议优先看 reference fidelity protocol、expert-lossless control arm、以及速度测试设置。如果时间有限，30 分钟内可以只看：Introduction、格式定义图表、quality evaluation protocol、serving throughput 结果。需要保留的判断是：这不是通用 LLM 量化结论，而是一个面向超大 MoE 流式部署的格式-系统联合设计。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a very specific but practical serving problem: for very large MoE models on consumer hardware, routed experts dominate the parameter footprint, exceed RAM, and must be streamed from SSD. In that regime, throughput is governed by bytes moved per token, while quality is governed by quantization damage. The central question is whether PTQTP-style two-plane ternary post-training quantization can be constrained into a uniform 9-level quantizer so that expert weights can be stored persistently in a single folded 4-bit code format, reused unchanged on disk, in cache, and at kernel input, with no transcoding in the serving path and acceptable quality loss.","importance":"Its direct relevance to pretraining is limited; this is primarily an inference-systems and quantized-serving paper. Still, it is useful for anyone working on large MoE systems. First, it shows that in SSD-streamed MoE serving, quantization format design is not just about perplexity. Persistent representation, I/O path, cache layout, and kernel compatibility matter just as much. Second, it presents a clear engineering trade-off: tying PTQTP’s two scales to a fixed 3:1 ratio reduces reconstruction flexibility, but buys a uniform 9-level code and a much cleaner end-to-end serving stack. That trade-off is relevant for future MoE-aware quantization and for training-time methods that may want to target deployment-native formats.","technical_approach":"$1f","experiments":"$20","reading_guide":"$21","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"做 MoE serving 的可以看：重点不是量化精度，而是“持久化格式=缓存=kernel 输入”这个系统取舍；只读格式设计和 kernel 路径，别把它当通用 PTQ 结论。","en":"Worth a skim if you care about MoE serving: the real idea is format unification across disk/cache/kernel, not a broadly convincing PTQ accuracy result."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Matteo Grella"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["MoE","quantization","PTQ","serving","SSD-streaming"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08910","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.08910","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.09444","title":"Depth-adaptive Inference of Looped Language Models via Continuous Depth Batching","abstract":"A main promise of looped language models (LMs) is depth-adaptive inference. By iterating a block of shared layers a variable number of times, the model can use less compute for \"easy\" tokens and more for \"hard\" ones. However, this adaptivity breaks standard batching: tokens in the same batch now require a different number of loops, so there is no unified forward pass, making efficient inference difficult. Standard inference frameworks like vLLM schedule on the token level and cannot handle this ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-10T11:20:14Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_09444.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL","cs.DC"],"summary":{"core_problem":"循环语言模型的深度自适应推理机制会为“简单”token分配更少的循环核迭代次数、为“困难”token分配更多迭代，从而实现更优的计算-精度权衡，但这种自适应特性会破坏标准batching流水线。vLLM等框架使用的token级调度器无法处理同一前向传播内不同token的循环次数差异，而模型中的非循环边界层（embedding、LM head、非共享层）又进一步增加了调度复杂度。此前的循环级调度方案缺少端到端实现，导致自适应循环LM的实际效率收益无法落地。","importance":"连续深度batching方案移除了深度自适应循环LM部署的核心系统瓶颈，将可变深度计算的理论算力节省转化为可实测的端到端吞吐、延迟收益。方案中明确的自适应计算架构与推理调度器的协同设计约束，对其他early exit、可变深度Transformer变体的落地也有通用参考价值。","technical_approach":"连续深度batching（CDB）以单次循环迭代为调度粒度，在前向传播内部将token路由到不同的模型计算阶段。方案提供两种执行模式：1）无填充（no-refill）模式：随着token完成循环退出，循环批次逐步缩小，所有退出的token等待整批循环完成后再统一执行coda阶段，实现复杂度最低；2）填充（refill）模式：通过优先级队列独立调度prelude、循环核、coda三个阶段，将边界层计算与循环步交错执行，循环层空出的计算槽位直接填入新token，最大化吞吐。方案设计了适配的KV cache布局，处理不同深度退出的token留下的cache空洞；引入提前一步的前瞻门（lookahead gate）做退出决策，将批次准备操作与GPU执行重叠，隐藏调度开销。此外，方案推导了自适应深度推理基于FLOPs的加速上界，通过硬件感知的roofline模型明确了填充模式能带来净收益的batch size区间。","experiments":"在Ouro 1.4B、Huginn 3.5B两个循环LM上的测试显示，CDB可达到理论最大加速比的99%，对应离线吞吐提升1.5-1.9倍，在线服务场景下归一化延迟降低45%-90%。消融实验量化了边界层计算占比、循环次数分布、batch size对实际收益的影响：大batch size下填充模式收益最高，小batch size下无填充模式的调度开销更低。","reading_guide":"泛读掌握核心思路即可。重点关注循环LM做batching的核心痛点拆解、两种CDB模式的设计权衡、roofline分析中架构参数（边界层占比、平均循环次数）和实际加速比的对应关系。如果不直接做自适应模型服务相关的系统工作，可以跳过底层系统实现细节。预计阅读时间25分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Depth-adaptive inference for looped language models allocates fewer recurrent core iterations to \"easy\" tokens and more to \"hard\" tokens, delivering improved compute-accuracy tradeoffs, but breaks standard batching pipelines. Token-level schedulers used in frameworks like vLLM cannot handle per-token variable loop counts within a single forward pass, and non-recurrent boundary stages (token embedding, LM head, unshared layers) add further scheduling complexity. Prior loop-level scheduling proposals lacked end-to-end implementations, leaving the practical efficiency gains of adaptive looped LMs unrealized.","importance":"Continuous Depth Batching removes the core systems bottleneck for deploying depth-adaptive looped LMs, translating theoretical compute savings from variable-depth computation into measurable end-to-end throughput and latency improvements. Ablations quantify how boundary stage cost, loop count distribution, and batch size affect realized gains: refill mode delivers the largest benefits at large batch sizes, while no-refill incurs lower overhead at small batch sizes.","reading_guide":"Skim for core ideas. agent harness”。这里的 harness 指可执行的外层脚手架：提示、工具调用流程、状态管理、错误恢复、子任务编排、代码逻辑等。难点在于，现有 agent benchmark 往往把基础模型能力、任务难度、harness 设计混在一起，导致你很难判断分数提升究竟来自更强的 base model，还是来自更好的 harness。作者把问题拆成三个必须同时满足的条件：任务要对 harness 改动敏感，而不是被底模能力主导；验证集和测试集要在这种“敏感性”上分布一致，避免在验证集上过拟合；评测过程要允许多轮、长时程的代码级迭代，而不是单轮 prompt search。","importance":"对基座研究的直接价值不在于提出了新的预训练方法，而在于把“agent self-improvement 的可测部分”单独剥离出来，形成了一个更干净的评测对象。对于做 pretrain 和 post-train 接口的人，这很重要：如果未来希望让模型在 deployment 中自动改 scaffold、自动做工具链优化，首先需要知道模型到底有没有这种内生能力，以及这种能力和底模规模、领域、policy model 是否解耦。Evo-Bench 的贡献量级更接近“评测协议和问题定义”而不是“方法突破”。它把 harness evolution 从零散案例推进到可比较、可复现实验，尤其强调 transfer：同一个演化出来的 harness 能否迁移到别的 policy model 上。这一点对研究‘结构性改进是否比 prompt trick 更稳’很有启发。","technical_approach":"方法核心是一个 harness-guided benchmark construction framework。不是先随便收一批任务再让模型去改 harness，而是先用辅助任务诱导出一组多样化 harness，再反过来用这些 harness 去筛任务，保留那些对 harness 差异真正有响应的任务。这样做的原因很直接：如果任务对 harness 不敏感，benchmark 最后测到的还是 base model strength。\n\n具体设计有三层。第一层是 harness-sensitive task construction。作者先构造或收集多个候选 harness，在固定 policy model 下运行，观察任务分数对 harness 变化的响应幅度，只保留能拉开不同 harness 表现的任务。第二层是 sensitivity-aware stratified splitting。作者不是按任务来源随机切分，而是按任务对 harness 的敏感性、难度和分布特征做分层切分，保证 validation 和 evaluation 在“可被 harness 改善”的统计属性上对齐，同时保持任务不重叠。这样可以减少 evolver 在验证集上学到 task-specific patch 的风险。第三层是 controlled long-horizon evolution。每次迭代中，evolver 只能看到截至当前轮的 validation 证据，包括历史 harness、聚合分数和任务级 outcome，然后修改可执行 harness 代码，再在固定 policy model 上重跑。这个设置把问题限定为：在固定底模下，模型能否根据失败模式做研究式迭代，而不是靠换底模取胜。\n\n从建模角度看，这篇工作的关键不是提出新的 evolver 算法，而是把 evolver、policy model、task suite 三者的耦合关系控制住。尤其值得注意的是，作者把 evolved harness 当作一种可迁移的 reasoning structure，而不是某个模型专属的 prompt。这个 framing 很像把外部 scaffold 视为一种非参数化、可执行的“后训练权重”。","experiments":"主实验在 Search、Office、General 三个 domain、五个已有 benchmark 上进行，评测了 9 个前沿闭源和开源模型作为 evolver。固定 policy model 后，最强的两个 evolver 是 GPT-5.6 Sol 和 Claude Opus 4.8，相对 seed harness 分别提升 16.6 和 16.1 分，接近人工工程 baseline 47.5。这个结果说明，当前强模型已经能做出实质性的 harness 改进，但距离人工设计仍有明显差距。\n\n分析部分比总分更有信息量。第一，evolution 存在明显 early saturation：前几轮很快找到有效结构，后续轮次反而更容易引入有害修改。这说明当前模型在“发现 obvious fix”上还可以，但在长期维护、回归测试、避免破坏已有能力上不稳定。第二，收益高度依赖 domain。Search 任务中，模型能通过复制或稳定化 web navigation 结构拿到很大增益；General tasks 中，某些自动合成的 harness 甚至能超过人工工程；Office 任务则更难，因为它依赖细粒度、流程特定的数据处理和操作顺序，模型不容易从有限 validation signal 中归纳出可靠 workflow。第三，迁移实验显示，合成 harness 对不同 policy model 具有鲁棒性，能在 Qwen、DeepSeek、GLM 等不同架构或家族上带来一致增益。这支持了作者的一个核心判断：演化出来的不是某个底模专属的 prompt hack，而是更一般的执行结构。\n\n虽然给出的全文片段没有完整表格数字，但从叙述可见，作者重点看的是相对 seed harness 的绝对分数提升、不同轮次的收益曲线、按 domain 分解的增益，以及跨 policy transfer 的稳定性。这几组实验共同支撑 benchmark 的有效性。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction，把作者定义的三个挑战记住：harness sensitivity、cross-split generalization、long-horizon evolution。然后直接看 benchmark construction 和主实验图表，重点确认作者是如何证明“这个 benchmark 测的是 harness evolution，而不是底模强弱”。\n\n建议优先看三类内容。第一，任务筛选和 split 构造细节，尤其是 sensitivity-aware stratification 的指标定义和实施方式；这是 benchmark 是否站得住的关键。第二，evolution trajectory 分析，关注 early saturation 的证据：是 validation gain 停滞、test gain 回落，还是代码修改复杂度上升导致回归。第三，cross-policy transfer 实验，这部分最接近基座研究者关心的问题：外部 scaffold 到底是不是一种可迁移的结构先验。\n\n如果时间有限，预计 25–35 分钟可完成有效泛读：读引言、方法总览、主结果表、trajectory 分析图、transfer 实验。若要深挖，值得进一步检查作者是否控制了 API budget、编辑步数、工具权限和执行环境一致性；这些因素会显著影响 benchmark 解释性。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not asking whether a model can solve agent tasks well. It asks whether a model can improve the executable agent harness itself over multiple rounds. Here, the harness includes prompts, tool-use logic, control flow, state handling, recovery code, and other scaffold components around a fixed policy model. The core difficulty is evaluation: most agent benchmarks entangle base-model strength, task difficulty, and harness quality, so score gains do not cleanly reflect harness-evolving ability. The paper isolates three requirements for a valid benchmark: tasks must be sensitive to harness changes rather than dominated by the base model; validation and test splits must align in that sensitivity to avoid split-specific overfitting; and the setup must support long-horizon iterative code refinement rather than one-shot prompt search.","importance":"Its main value for foundation-model researchers is as an evaluation and problem-definition contribution. It turns harness evolution into a measurable capability instead of a collection of anecdotes. This matters for the pretrain/post-train interface: if future systems are expected to improve their own scaffolds, tool chains, or execution policies during deployment, we need a benchmark that separates intrinsic scaffold-improvement ability from raw model strength. The paper also highlights transferability: whether an evolved harness helps multiple policy models, not just the one used during search. f","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-08T17:07:11Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_08239.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"多步 LLM agent 中的逐步骤模型路由（per-step model switching / routing）几乎都用重放日志轨迹的方式离线评测，但 agent 是闭环系统，替换某一步的模型会改变后续状态和动作，这种 replay evaluator 是否可信一直缺乏定量测量。","importance":"对 agent 训练与评测方法有直接影响，尤其和 pretrain-posttrain 接口相关：当前大量 routing、budgeted inference、模型选择策略是基于离线 replay 打分得出结论的，而论文指出这种评测在多步闭环任务中系统性偏误很大。对长期推理、tool use、代码 agent 这类逐步决策系统的评估 protocol 设计有现实价值。","technical_approach":"提出 branching rollouts 协议：在 高 +0.25 到 +0.66 归一化编辑距离；早期 swap 有 74–77% 在 fork 后第一步就分歧，仅 3–8% 的 replay 状态仍然有效；5 次 outcome flip 全部出现在 swap 分支，359 个 control 分支中为 0。用标准 replay evaluator 评同样的固定切换策略时，与真实 outcome 几乎正交，所有与成功相关的结果都被误判。另外观察到：升级模型（小换大）分歧更快，后期降级（大换小）接近 control 行为；强模型在 50 步预算内还存在“thoroughness tax”——花更多步骤探索但未必更多提交。即便是同模型、温度 0 的“确定性”设置，不同量化/serving 栈（FP8 4B vs AWQ 14B）也会产生很大 control 分歧，说明 same-model almost always evaluated by replaying logged trajectories, but agents are closed-loop systems: substituting a model at one step changes future states and actions. The validity of this replay-style evaluation protocol has not been quantitatively measured.","importance":"Directly relevant to agent training and evaluation, and to the pretrain–posttrain interface more broadly. A large body of work on routing, budgeted inference, and model selection draws conclusions from offline replay scoring, and this paper shows that such evaluation is systematically misleading in multi-step closed-loop settings. The result matters for evaluation protocol design in long-horizon reasoning, tool use, and coding agents.","technical_approach":"The paper introduces a branching-rollout protocol: at controlled steps in live SWE-bench Verified trajectories, it forks execution, rebuilds the Docker environment, replays the action prefix with high fidelity, and then continues one branch with the swapped model and a paired same-model control branch that absorbs sampling noise, batching effects, and environment-replay drift. Multi-step sampling for diffusion/flow matching heads and classifier-free guidance introduce non-trivial compute overhead at every generation step, making it difficult to simultaneously meet audio quality, speaker similarity, and low-latency requirements.","importance":"This work falls in the domain of speech language modeling and cross-modal generation. It validates the feasibility of a compact architecture combining semantically aligned causal VAE, patch-level autoregression, and flow matching heads. The proposed guidance-step distillation design provides general reference for inference acceleration in joint AR+diffusion modeling paradigms, eliminating the need for two separate distillation passes for CFG removal and sampling step reduction.","technical_approach":"The system is built on four core components: 1) A causal σ-VAE operating at 12.5 Hz frame rate, trained with a semantic alignment loss against a frozen WavLM teacher to avoid posterior collapse of continuous latents, reducing autoregressive sequence length while preserving reconstruction quality; 2) A lightweight distilled ECAPA speaker encoder, with explicit speaker conditioning injected to improve zero-shot voice cloning similarity; 3) A patching scheme with 2 frames per patch, where a causal backbone autoregressively predicts sequences of continuous patches, and a bidirectional flow matching head models within-patch local acoustic dependencies; 4) Guidance-step distillation, which trains a single student model on interval-averaged velocities along the CFG-guided teacher trajectory, absorbing both CFG branch computation and multi-step numerical integration into a single forward pass at inference time, with no extra guidance calculation or multi-step sampling required.","experiments":"The 0.2B parameter model achieves comparable intelligibility and speaker similarity on LibriSpeech and Seed-TTS benchmarks, and is preferred over same-sized baselines in subjective listening tests. After distillation, mean first-audio latency drops by 23.3%, real-time factor improves by 40.8%, and synthesis quality remains nearly identical to the undistilled base model. Ablations confirm the semantic alignment objective significantly improves intelligibility, explicit speaker conditioning boosts zero-shot cloning performance, and controlled experiments on latent rate and patch size quantify the tradeoff between reconstruction fidelity, autoregressive sequence length, and within-patch generation difficulty.","reading_guide":"Skim the problem breakdown in the introduction, the design of guidance-step distillation, and ablation tables for latent rate and patch size. counterfactual/causal utility（没有这些观测就不行）。这类标准一旦成立，会改变工具数据合成的配方：先筛题（必须需要工具），再合成（让调用更结构化），最后用“工具观测是否提升基座模型成功率”做验证。它更像是“数据质量定义与可操作的过滤器”，而不是一个新模型结构；价值主要在数据管线与可复用的判别准则。","technical_approach":"OpenVisTool 给出一个三阶段轨迹合成与筛选框架，并把“可教性”(instructive)形式化为两个条件的交集：\n1) Outcome validity：轨迹在任务评估器下最终答案正确，避免把错误推理链当监督。\n2) Causal utility：固定同一张图 I 与问题 q，用一个固定 probe/base model 做对照实验——(a) 不给工具观测/工具调用的 no-tool baseline；(b) 给定该轨迹的工具调用与观测（相当于把外部证据喂给模型）。只有当 (b) 的成功率显著高于 (a)，才认为这条轨迹的工具部分“做了真实工作”。\n围绕这一定义，管线分三步：\n- Difficulty Screening：先筛掉“强模型不用工具也能稳定答对”的题，避免老师在简单题上被提示词驱动而硬调用工具，从源头减少伪监督。\n- Domain-Specific Trajectory Synthesis：按域设计结构化调用策略（文中覆盖 chart/table/GUI grounding/visual search/web-to-HTML），目标是让 teacher 生成更连贯、与任务结构一致的 tool trace，而不是自由发挥的工具噪声。\n- Supervision Verification：同时检查 outcome validity 与 causal utility；后者用 probe model 的成功率差来估计。这个设计的关键取舍是：不试图做“人类式解释一致性”判断，而是用可重复的、模型可执行的反事实效用测试，把筛选目标对齐到“工具观测是否提升可回答性”。","experiments":"论文构建 OpenVisTool-42K，覆盖五个视觉推理域并共享一套视觉工具。作者在 4B–27B 的四个 backbone Recently, there has been growing evidence that it is also an important ingredient in LLM reinforcement learning recipes that can significantly impact downstream performance. Many existing methods control exploration in the action-space, for example, using temperature scaling. As a result, high-reward trajectories are rarely sampled. For grouped advantage algorithms like GRPO, zero-advantage groups emerge when all rollouts of a prompt share the same reward, causing training stalls and wasted compute.","importance":"This work introduces parameter-space exploration to LLM RLVR training for the first time, providing a new control lever for exploration that is complementary to temperature scaling and entropy regularization. It has direct practical implications for mainstream GRPO-style RL post-training pipelines, especially in alleviating under-exploration and zero-advantage groups on hard examples.","technical_approach":"The proposed Perturbed Parameter Policy Optimization (3PO) framework maintains an approximate posterior over model parameters. At each training step, multiple perturbed policies are sampled by injecting learned noise into the parameters, which directly modifies logits and reorders token distributions — a capability beyond temperature scaling. Three variants are designed: B3PO draws one weight perturbation per gradient step, which is simple but limits group diversity; M3PO computes advantages separately for M independent weight perturbations before a single parameter update; C3PO splits one GRPO group across N independently perturbed weights to maximize within-group diversity. Noise scale is adapted via variational learning, without requiring additional rollouts.","experiments":"Validation is conducted on OLMo-3-1025-7B and Qwen2.5-Math-7B across mathematical reasoning and code generation tasks. C3PO achieves the best average performance across both model families, improving downstream results and accelerating convergence over the AdamW baseline without increasing rollout count. Multi-sample variants (M3PO/C3PO) consistently outperform single-sample B3PO and rescue more zero-advantage groups than action-space baselines such as temperature scaling. Gains are concentrated on harder benchmarks including AIME and LiveCodeBench, indicating that parameter exploration is more effective at resolving exploration bottlenecks on difficult tasks.","reading_guide":"Skim-level read, ~15–20 minutes. That separation is then read as evidence that the score will also catch the attacks that succeed. Harmful intent is a property of the prompt. Jailbreak success is an outcome produced later by a particular target model, decoding policy, and judge. A filter tuned on a score that measures the wrong quantity spends its false positive budget on attacks","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-10T14:05:32Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_09624.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.CR"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是“如何做更强的越狱检测”，而是一个更基础的测量问题：很多内部安全分数是在生成前、只看 prompt 的条件下打分，并用“能区分 harmful vs benign prompt”来证明有效；但部署时，人们往往把这个分数当成“能拦住成功越狱”的证据。论文指出这两者不是同一个随机变量。前者是 prompt 的有害意图 H(x)，后者是目标模型、解码策略和 judge 共同决定的实现结果 Ym(x)。如果一个分数只对 H 有构念效度，却被拿去做 Ym 的阈值决策，就可能把拦截预算浪费在本来就不会成功的攻击上，反而放过真正会成功的包装式越狱。","importance":"对预训练本身不是直接方法贡献，但对“如何解释内部表征、如何验证 probe、如何把内部信号转成部署决策”有很强的方法论价值。它提醒人们：内部 probe 的训练标签和部署目标必须一致，不能把 representation-level harmfulness 当作 outcome-level jailbreak success 的替代。这个问题和预训练研究里常见的 probe 误读、OOD 失效、代理指标错配是同一类。尤其对做 activation probe、safety head、representation auditing、甚至训练中间信号监控的人，这篇文章给了一个很清楚的反例：construct validity 不会自动推出 outcome validity，更不会自动推出 decision validity。","technical_approach":"方法上有两个层次。第一层是估计对象的拆分。作者显式区分 harmful intent H、是否带 wrapper 的 W、以及目标模型在固定 judge 下是否真的输出有害内容的 Ym，并分别讨论 ranking、calibration、threshold transfer 三种效度，而不是只报一个 AUROC 就结束。这个拆分很重要，因为很多安全检测工作把这些量混在一起。\n\n第二层是测量设计。作者认为现有 attention-based detector 常从 prompt-dependent 的位置读取信号，wrapper 一旦改变模板结构，既改变了被测内容，也改变了读数坐标，导致分数变化无法归因。为此他们提出 Active Attention Probing, AAP：在 system region 插入一个短 probe embedding，形成固定、与 prompt 内容无关的参考坐标，再读取该 probe 位置上的 attention response，拼接多层多头特征后用 XGBoost 映射到 [0,1] 分数。核心不是“probe token 本身更强”，而是把比较基准固定住，使 plain prompt 和 wrapped prompt 的差异更可解释。\n\n具体实现上，主设定使用 K=3 的 probe rows，只在 BeaverTails 训练集上训练并冻结；后续所有审计都不再用 JailbreakHub、WildJailbreak、JailbreakBench 去挑 probe、挑 head 或调 harmfulness readout，尽量避免在攻击集上做选择偏差。除了 AAP 主通道，作者还做了多种替代读出：rare token、common token、norm-matched、system span、passive attention、refusal logit channel，用来检验结论是否依赖某一种坐标或特征家族。论文还设置了一个 outcome-supervised positive control，证明如果直接用 success label 训练，模型是能学到与结果更相关的信号的；因此问题不在于“内部表征完全没信息”，而在于“harmfulness-trained score 被过度解释”。","experiments":"最关键的结果有三组。\n\n第一组是 harmfulness 与 realized success 的分离。在 Llama 目标模型上，给有害请求加 wrapper 后，harmful generation rate 从 0.05 提高到 0.27，说明包装确实让更多攻击成功；但同一批样本上的 harmful intent AUROC 却从 0.936 降到 0.803。更强的一点是，在 wrapped harmful prompts 内部，这个分数把成功攻击排在失败攻击后面，outcome AUROC 只有 0.220，已经不是“弱相关”，而是明显反向排序。\n\n第二组是稳健性审计。作者声称这种 anti-ranking 现象并不依赖单一测量通道，而是在不同 channel、不同 target、不同 attack family、不同独立 judge 下都能看到。也就是说，这不是某个 probe token 或某个 head 的偶然坏例子，而是标签错配带来的系统性问题。\n\n第三组是部署含义。论文不只看 AUROC，还看在固定 benign false positive budget 下，阈值如何分配拦截容量。结论是：如果阈值是按 harmfulness score 调的，它会优先拦下那些“看起来更有害、但其实不会成功”的攻击，而把真正会成功的 wrapped jailbreak 留在阈值下方。作者还区分了 ranking loss、probability miscalibration、threshold transfer failure，说明即便一个分数在原分布上校准得还可以，到了 wrapper shift 后也可能同时在排序和阈值迁移上失效。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先抓住问题定义，再看方法细节。先读 Introduction 和 Measurement Setting，重点看 H(x)、W(x)、Ym(x) 的定义，以及 construct validity / outcome validity / decision validity 的区分。这部分决定了整篇文章在批评什么。\n\n然后看 Audit Design，重点理解为什么 passive attention readout 会被 wrapper 改变坐标，以及 AAP 如何通过 system region 的固定 probe 解决这个混淆。这里不需要把所有公式都推一遍，但要看清楚 probe 插入位置、读出的 attention response 是什么、XGBoost 只是 readout 而不是主要创新。\n\n实验部分建议优先看 Figure 1 和所有 plain-vs-wrapped、harmfulness-vs-outcome 的主结果表。阅读时不要只盯 AUROC，要同时看 calibration 和 threshold transfer，因为作者真正关心的是部署决策。若时间有限，30-40 分钟足够抓住主线；若做 safety probing 或内部表征研究，建议再看 appendix 里的 channel ablation、head selection、positive control 细节，那里能看出作者如何避免在攻击集上过拟合结论。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a measurement mismatch rather than a new jailbreak defense. Many internal safety scores are computed before generation from the prompt alone, and are validated by separating harmful from benign prompts. In deployment, however, these scores are often treated as evidence that a prompt will successfully jailbreak a target model. The paper argues that these are different estimands: harmful intent H(x) is a property of the prompt, while realized jailbreak success Ym(x) depends on the target model, decoding policy, and judge. A score that is valid for H can still be invalid, or even anti-correlated, for Ym.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it is highly relevant to representation probing, internal-signal interpretation, and deployment-facing validation. The main contribution is methodological clarity: construct validity for a probe target does not imply outcome validity for a downstream event, and neither implies decision validity under thresholding. That lesson transfers directly to pretraining research whenever internal activations, probes, or surrogate metrics are used as evidence for a stronger claim than the label actually supports. We present U N M ASK, a ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-10T07:31:08Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_09209.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文要解决的是文本分类器中的伪相关发现问题：数据里某些表面模式和标签相关，但不一定被模型真正使用；已有方法常常只能发现数据层面的相关性，或需要人工指定偏置特征，无法自动区分“数据中存在”和“模型依赖”。","importance":"对基座预训练的直接贡献有限，因为实验主体是 NLI、评论毒性分类和偏好数据上的分类器分析。但它对预训练数据审计有方法启发：把候选数据 artifact 表达成可执行的布尔函数，再用反事实编辑验证模型是否真的依赖该特征，比只看词频、互信息或 benchmark 分组更可靠。这个思路可迁移到预训练语料质量分析、指令数据偏置检测、reward/preference 数据 shortcut 诊断。","technical_approach":"UnMask 分三步。第一步是自动发现：用 LLM 在不看标签的训练样本上提出候选表面模式，并要求输出自然语言描述、类别标签、简短理由和可执行 boolean expression。作者刻意不用自由文本特征，而是用确定性的布尔表达式，目的是让后续统计过滤、反事实验证和重加权都能复用同一个特征定义。为降低长上下文幻觉，样本被分 batch 查询，再聚合候选。第二步是统计验证：先做表达式和描述层面的去重，再用两阶段 replication protocol 过滤真正和标签稳定相关的特征。第三步是因果验证：对每个候选特征生成最小反事实编辑，移除表面模式但尽量保持语义标签不变，然后比较编辑前后的模型预测变化。只有当预测发生系统性变化时，才认为模型利用了该 shortcut。最后，这些布尔表达式还能直接作为 group label，用于 annotation-free Deep Feature Reweighting，避免人工 demographic 或 bias annotation。","experiments":"实验覆盖 MNLI 上的 BERT、RoBERTa，以及多个 NLI 相关 benchmark；还扩展到 CivilComments-WILDS 和 RewardBench2 偏好数据。文中报告的关键现象是：UnMask 能恢复 NLI 中已知的 lexical overlap 和 negation bias；相关性分析看不出的架构差异可以通过反事实验证暴露出来，例如 BERT 会利用某些 contradiction 相关特征，而 RoBERTa 对这些特征不敏感；在 sentiment 场景中方法返回零个被模型实际利用的特征，对这些非因果特征做 debiasing 也不会带来收益。CivilComments 上，用自动挖出的 group 做 DFR，可以接近手工标注 group 的 DFR 效果。提供内容未包含完整数值表，因此不应过度解读提升幅度。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，预计 20–30 分钟。重点看方法部分的三个接口：LLM 如何生成 executable boolean expression，统计过滤如何避免把偶然相关当作 artifact，反事实编辑如何把 dataset-level correlation 和 model-level reliance 分开。若关心预训练数据治理，可以重点看 boolean expression 作为统一 artifact 表示的设计，而不是具体 NLI 结果。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific gap in shortcut analysis for text classifiers: surface patterns may correlate with labels in the dataset, but correlation alone does not show that a trained model actually uses them. Prior methods often require humans to specify the bias vocabulary or stop at dataset-level discovery.","importance":"The direct relevance to foundation-model pretraining is moderate, since the main experiments are on classifiers rather than base LM training. The useful idea is methodological: represent candidate artifacts as executable boolean functions, then reuse the same representation for statistical filtering, counterfactual causal verification, and debiasing. This is a cleaner interface than ad hoc keyword lists and could be adapted to pretraining corpus audits, instruction-data bias checks, and reward/preference-data shortcut diagnosis.","technical_approach":"UnMask has three stages. First, an LLM proposes candidate surface patterns from unlabeled training examples and returns a natural-language description plus an executable boolean expression. Labels are withheld at this stage so that candidate generation is not label-conditioned. The boolean expression is the central design choice, because it makes feature evaluation deterministic across discovery, verification, and mitigation. Candidates are generated in mini-batches, deduplicated using exact normalization and semantic clustering, and then statistically filtered with a two-phase replication protocol. Second, the method causally verifies model reliance by generating minimal counterfactual edits that remove the surface pattern while preserving the semantic label, and then measuring paired prediction shifts. Third, the same boolean expressions are used as group labels for annotation-free Deep Feature Reweighting.","experiments":"The paper evaluates on MNLI with BERT and RoBERTa, several NLI benchmarks, CivilComments-WILDS, and RewardBench2 preference data. The main reported findings are qualitative but informative: UnMask recovers known lexical-overlap and negation biases; it exposes an architecture-level difference where BERT exploits some contradiction-associated features while RoBERTa does not; on sentiment it returns no exploited features, and debiasing these non-causal features does not help. On CivilComments, automatically mined groups can support DFR with performance close to hand-labeled groups. The supplied text does not include full numeric tables, so exact effect sizes should not be inferred.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. The main lesson is that long-document capability is not only a context-length problem. A model also needs mechanisms for selective reading, stateful memory, evidence attribution, and revisiting previous findings. DocAtlas provides an environment that can be used both at inference time and during RL, which makes it more useful for training compact open VLM agents than a prompt-only tool-use recipe.","technical_approach":"DocAtlas instantiates a mutable document harness. For each document-question pair, the environment maintains the document, a hierarchical tree, a structured note store, and the set of explored pages. The agent produces a trajectory of tool calls and ends with a Finish action. Search operates over the current mutable state rather than a fixed index. Read exposes selected pages through markdown, crops, and full page images. Note records source-attributed findings and writes them back into the tree and note store. Review retrieves earlier notes when later reasoning needs them. This design lets later retrieval depend on earlier evidence, which is the main difference from static RAG. It also differs from prompt-only document agents because the same action interface can be used for RL. Compared with DocDancer, the agent is not just a text controller that delegates visual understanding to an external VLM; the VLM policy can be trained inside the same environment.","experiments":"On MMLongBench-Doc, DocAtlas with GPT-5.4 reaches 71.4%, compared with a reported human-expert reference of 65.8%. 和轨迹构造细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific failure mode in RL training for search agents: standard rewards cannot tell apart necessary retrieval from redundant retrieval, nor can they distinguish answers that truly depend on retrieved evidence from answers the model already knew and happened to retrieve before answering. Outcome-only rewards collapse these behaviors into the same “correct” label. Internal signals such as entropy, confidence, or information gain are cheap, but they mostly track uncertainty rather than grounding. The goal is to build a low-cost reward that measures policy-relative retrieval necessity and answer-level evidence reliance without process annotations or external LLM judges.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model research is mostly through training-signal design rather than search as an application. The useful idea is to turn expensive counterfactual supervision into amortized lightweight readouts that can be used during online RL. That pattern matters beyond search agents: scalable reward construction, reward-policy mismatch under policy drift, and restoring learning signal when all samples in a GRPO group are correct but behavior quality still differs. The contribution is solid and practical, though not a new modeling paradigm.","technical_approach":"$38","experiments":"$39","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper. Focus on three parts. Second, read the reward definition and the normalization-aware credit assignment, especially how it handles zero-advantage all-correct groups under GRPO-style updates. Third, read the periodic refitting experiments to understand how quickly the readouts drift and why static reward estimators are insufficient.\n\nIf time is limited, prioritize Figure 1, Figure 2, the calibration procedure for the two readouts, and the diagnostics on estimator fidelity, tie-breaking, and drift. Measuring and improving these social skills is hard because, unlike math or logic, social interaction offers no objective ground truth: evaluations fall back on LLM judges, which are costly, subjective, and noisy, and models get no reliable signal to learn from. To address both, we first introduce Social Gym, an environment of 21 multi-agent social games (e.g., Wer","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-10T05:12:56Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2608_09128.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.MA"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的是一个评测与改进信号问题：LLM 在多智能体社交互动中的能力很难测，也很难改。难测，是因为很多 social reasoning 评测依赖静态问答或 LLM judge，前者测不到多轮互动，后者主观、昂贵且噪声大。难改，是因为没有稳定、可验证的奖励信号，模型很难从社交博弈中积累可迁移策略。作者因此把问题落在“规则可验证的多智能体社交游戏”上，用游戏结果而不是裁判打分来定义能力，并进一步测试模型能否通过自我复盘生成 playbook，在不更新参数的情况下提升社交表现。","importance":"对预训练本身的直接影响有限，但对 post-train、agent training 和可验证环境设计有启发。它提供了一类比开放式对话更干净的社会性评测环境：多轮、交互式、覆盖多种社交结构，而且 reward 由规则决定。对于做 RLVR、agent self-play、合成训练信号的人，这类环境有现实意义，因为它把“社会推理”从主观 judge 迁移到可计算 outcome。SPaRTan 的价值不在于方法新奇度很高，而在于它展示了自然语言 playbook 作为非参数记忆的边界：强模型能从自博弈轨迹中提炼策略并迁移，较弱开源模型则几乎吃不到这种收益。这对理解 in-context self-improvement 的容量条件有参考价值。","technical_approach":"工作分两部分。第一部分是 Social Gym，一个统一的多智能体社交游戏环境。设计原则有两个：一是每个游戏都必须有算法可验证的 outcome，例如胜负、得分、生存状态，这样评测和后续训练都能使用明确 reward；二是游戏集合要覆盖不同社交智能维度，而不是只测单一博弈。系统层面，它扩展了 SOTOPIA 的环境循环，支持任意 N-agent 交互；用有限状态机管理复杂 phase transition；用 visibility layer 控制 public、team-private、private 三类消息可见性，从而显式考察 hidden state 推断、Theory-of-Mind、联盟与欺骗等能力。21 个游戏按信息结构和通信模式组织，覆盖重复矩阵博弈、资源分配、隐藏状态、隐藏身份、自由对话等多类场景，并通过统一 Elo tournament 形成跨游戏 leaderboard。\n\n第二部分是 SPaRTan，一个 training-free 的自改进循环。流程是：模型先进行 self-play；然后读取自己的对局轨迹，写出可迁移的 strategic playbook；再把这个 playbook 注入 system prompt，参与后续游戏。作者把它类比为 in-context fine-tuning：训练数据是自己的交互轨迹，更新后的“参数”是自然语言规则。这个方法选择自然语言 playbook 而不是直接做参数更新，有两个现实原因：一是避免昂贵且不稳定的 RL 或 SFT；二是便于测试策略是否能跨游戏、跨角色、跨模型迁移。论文还考察了强模型生成的 playbook 蒸馏给弱模型的效果，这使它不只是一个单模型自反思实验。","experiments":"Benchmark 部分评测了 7 个 LLM，在 21 个社交游戏上做 tournament，并给出 per-game win rate 和总体 Elo 排名。总体上 GPT-5-mini 排名最高，但作者强调不存在“所有游戏都强”的统一冠军，很多模型在不同游戏和不同角色上会发生明显 ranking inversion。这一点很关键：它说明 social reasoning 不是单轴能力，隐藏信息推断、谈判、欺骗、合作、声誉管理等子能力并不总是一起提升。\n\nSPaRTan 的实验分四类：同一游戏内多轮反思迭代 R1–R4；从单个源游戏向未见游戏迁移；多游戏 hold-one-out 迁移；强模型 playbook 蒸馏给弱模型。主要结论是，GPT-5-mini 生成的 playbook 能显著提升模型在非对称游戏中结构性较弱的一方，并且这种策略可以跨游戏迁移，也能迁移给较弱学生模型；但收益明显依赖模型容量，在开源 Qwen3-32B 上基本消失。这个结果很有意思，因为它说明“把经验写成规则”并不自动等于“模型能用好这些规则”。需要足够强的语言理解、情境匹配和执行能力，playbook 才能转化为稳定收益。\n\n从全文片段看，作者还强调 Social Gym 的 outcome 是 verifiable reward，因此不仅适合 leaderboard，也适合后续 RL value estimation 和 RLVR 训练。若看完整论文，值得重点留意不同游戏类别上的分项结果，以及 playbook 在 source-target game pair 上的迁移矩阵；这些比总 Elo 更能说明问题。","reading_guide":"泛读时，先看 Social Gym 的环境设计，而不是先看排行榜。关键是弄清楚作者如何把“社交能力”转成可验证的交互任务：FSM、消息可见性、hidden information、统一 reward 接口，这些是 benchmark 可信度的基础。然后看 21 个游戏的分类方式，理解每一类到底在测什么。\n\n接着看 SPaRTan。重点不是流程本身，而是 playbook multi-agent settings. Social ability is hard to measure because static ToM-style probes miss sustained interaction, while open-ended evaluations often rely on LLM judges that are subjective, noisy, and expensive. It is also hard to improve because there is rarely a stable, verifiable learning signal. The paper addresses both by moving to rule-governed social games with algorithmically verifiable outcomes, then testing whether models can improve through self-play trajectory reflection and natural-language strategy extraction without parameter updates.","importance":"The direct relevance to pretraining is limited, but the paper is useful for post-training, agent training, and environment design. That matters for RLVR and self-play style training, where reward quality is often the bottleneck. SPaRTan is also informative as a study of non-parametric self-improvement: strong models can extract and reuse strategic rules from their own trajectories, while weaker open-weight models gain little.
